Vādhūla Zrautasūtra 6-7 (Jyotiṣṭoma)

Checked: N1(98.07.014); C checked (Caland handwritten copy, p.45-p.106) 


6.1.1 [N1,56a1;M156;T1,184] 

 6.1.1.1. dīkṣiṣyamāṇas sambhārān sambharate: dve payasī dve kṛṣṇājine dve dīkṣitavasane sadyomathitan navanītaṃ sadyaḫpiṣṭam āñjanaṃ [N1,56a2] satūlā śareṣīkā dvayāni darbhapuñjīlāni trīṇy anyāny ekaviṃśatir anyāni yoktrañ ca mekhalā ca (C72) naskūjanī ca kṛṣṇaviṣānā caudumbaraś ca daṇḍaś ca musalaś ca vratapradānā.
 6.1.1.2. spaṣṭe devayajane [N1,56a3] prācīnavaṃśā śālā bhavati  dikṣv atīkāśā nakṣatrapathena sammitā dakṣiṇataś ca-uttarataś ca dvārau.
 6.1.1.3. keśaśmaśru vapate. nakhāni nikṛntata. āśiśatā bhūtvā snāto 'abhyakto [N1,56a4]
 6.1.1.4. "bhadrād abhi śreyaḥ prehi(1.2.3m)"-iti kṛtaśālan devayajanam abhiprayāti "bṛhaspatiḥ puraetā te astv'' ity 
 6.1.1.5.  ''athem ava sya vara ā pṛthivyā(1.2.3n)" ity apareṇa śālām avasyaty "āre śatrūn kṛṇuhi sarvavīra" ity [N1,56a5]
6.1.1.6. "edam aganma devayajanaṃ pṛthivyā(1.2.3o)" iti devayajanam abhimantrayate ÔÔviśve devā=sam iṣā mademaÕÕ-iti .
 6.1.1.7. kaṇTakavatīñ cākaṇTakāñ ca śākhe ādāya-"apeta vīta vi ca sarpatāta(4.2.4a)" ity āntaś śālād udajati prācīnaṃ ca śālāyai devayajanād ÔÔye Ôatra stha purāṇā ye ca nūtanā adād idaṃ+lokam asmāÕÕ ity 
 6.1.1.8. utkare śākhe nyasya caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte [N1,56a7] 
6.1.1.9.  taj juhoti śyonā pṛthivi no bhava"-ity audumbaryai kāle darbhastambe hiraṇyam upāsya stambāhutim  "anṛkṣarā niveśanī yacchā naś śarma saprathā svāhā"-iti.
 6.1.1.10. pūrvayā dvārā śālāṃ prapādya[N1,56a8]  mathitvāgnīn vihṛtya-anvāhāryapacane pitṛbhyo dattvā putre vā bhrātari varṇam upanayaty. 
 5.1.1.16. caturaṅgulyudhāni saṃsādyāpaḫ praṇīyāgnāvaiṣṇavam [N1,56a9] ekādaśakapālaṃ dīkṣanīyaṃ nirvapati  
 6.1.1.12. nirupte prāṇagrahāṁś ca pṛśnīṁś ca vyācaṣṭe 
 6.1.1.13. tasya haviṣkṛtā vācaṃ visṛjya caturgṛhītena saptahotāram āhavanīye juhoti dvādaśartūn "madhave svāhā mādhavāya svāhā"-ity etāñ.
 6.1.1.14. chṛte 'asmin stambayajur harati [N1,56b1] vediṃ parigṛhṇāti  karaṇaṃ japati  
 6.1.1.15. prasūta uttaraṃ parigrāhaṃ parigṛhṇāti  
 6.1.1.16. yoyupitvāvaskabhya saṃpreṣyati "prokṣaṇīr āsādaya-idhmābarhir upasādaya sruvañ ca srucaś ca sammṛḍḍhi patnīn sannahyājyena-udehi"-ity. 
 6.1.1.17. etat kurvanti  
 6.1.1.18. srucas sammṛjyājyāni gṛhṇīta  idhmābarhiḫ prokṣati  vedim prokṣaty  āsannam prokṣaty 
 6.1.1.19. apo ninīya prastaraṃ gṛhṇāti 
6.1.1.20.  stṛṇāti barhiḫ  
 6.1.1.21. prāṅ utsṛpya paridhīn paridadhāti [N1,56b3] samidhāv ādadhāti 
 6.1.1.22. paridhiṣu vācayitvāgniṃ yunakty. 
 6.1.1.23. āgatya tiraścī sādayati  tiraścyoH prastaraṃ  prastare juhūṃ  barhiṣītare 
 6.1.1.24. samāsanne [N1,56b4] apānagrahāṁś ca pṛśnīṁś ca vyācaṣṭe. 
 6.1.1.25. "dhruvā asadan, yan me agna" iti samabhimṛśya daśahotāraṃ vyācaṣṭe. 
 6.1.1.26. 'agnaye samidhyamānāyānuvāca āha-
 6.1.1.27. -anūktāsu sāmidhenīṣu sruveṇāghāram āghāryāha-"agnīd agnin tris-tris sammṛḍḍhi"-iti. 
 6.1.1.28. srugbhyām uttaram asaṁsparśayann atyākramya dhruvāṃ samajya sādayitvā pravaraṃ pravṛṇīte [N1,56b7] 
 6.1.1.29. sīdati hotā 
 6.1.1.30. prasūta srucāv ādatte 
 6.1.1.31. prayājān yajati 
 6.1.1.32. prayājān iṣṭvātyākramya dhruvām abhighārayati  havir  atha-upabhṛtam 
 6.1.1.33. ājyabhāgau yajaty  [N1,56b8] 
 6.1.1.34. agnāviṣṇū haviṣo yajati 
 6.1.1.35. sviṣṭakṛtaṃ yajaty 
 6.1.1.36. atyākramya yathāyatanaṁ srucau sādayitvā prāśitram avadyaty 
 6.1.1.37. apa upaspṛśya-iḍām avadyaty 
 6.1.1.38. upahūtāyām iḍāyām [N1,56b9] agnīdha ādadhāti
 6.1.1.39. prāśnanti  mārjayante  pariharanti 
 6.1.1.40. brahmaṇe prāśite prāśitre santatim anu hotur āhutiñ juhoti 
 6.1.1.41. "brahman prasthāsyāma" ity anūyājān (C73) yajaty  
 6.1.1.42. āgatya vājavatībhyāṁ srucau vyūhati 
 6.1.1.43. śaṃyunā prastaraparidhi samprakirati 
 6.1.1.44.  paretya patnīs saṃyājayanti 
 6.1.1.45. phalīkaraṇahomaṃ hutvā prāṅ etya [N1,56b10] nidhāya srucam udañco niṣkrāmanty 
 6.1.1.46. uttareṇa śālām pariśrita eta eva dīkṣaṇīyās sambhārā upakLptā bhavanti udakumbho 'ahataṃ vāsaḥ 

6.1.2  [N1,56b10; M158;T1,187] [dīkṣā] 

 6.1.2.1. udakumbho 'ahataṃ vāsas sadyomathitan [N1,57a1] navanītaṃ sadyaḫpiṣṭam āñjanaṃ satūlā śareṣīkā-ubhayāni darbhapuñjīlāni kṣuraḫ
 6.1.2.2. prapādyaitat pariśritam "āpa undantu jīvasa(1.2.1a=?)" ity asya daksiṇaṃ godānam unakti [N1,57a2] "dīrghāyutvāya varcasa" ity.
 6.1.2.3. "oṣadhe Êtrāyasva-enam(1.2.1b=)" iti tribhir darbhapuñjīlaiḫ prācīnatūlair adhinidadhāti  [cf.5.2.1.4]
 6.1.2.4. śvadhite mā-enaṃ hiṃṣīr(1.2.1c=)" iti svadhitinā "devaśrur etāni pravapa"-iti pravapati  [cf.6.2.1.9] [N1,57a3]
 6.1.2.5. śvasty uttarāṇy aśīyā(1.2.1d=)"-ity uttarāṇi pratyabhimṛśaty 
 6.1.2.6. evam evottaraṃ gadānaṃ vapati 
 6.1.2.7. puroptaṃ satkṣuragandhi karoty 
 6.1.2.8. apa āśayaty "āpo asmān mātaraś śundhantv(1.2.1f1)" iti  [N1,57a4]
 6.1.2.9. "ghṛtena no ghṛtapuvaḫ punantv(1.2.1f2)" ity adbhir abhiṣiñcate 
 6.1.2.10. "viśvam asmat pravahantu ripram(1.2.1f3)" iti sravantīr anvavekṣata 
 6.1.2.11. "ud ābhyaś śucir ā pūta emi(1.2.1f4)"-ity udaṅṅ utsarpati
 6.1.2.12. śomasya tanūr asi tanuvam me pāhi(1.2.1g=)"-ity [N1,57a5] ahataṃ vāsaḫ paridhatte.
 6.1.2.13. "mahīnāṃ payo 'asi varcodhā asi varco mayi dhehi(1.2.1h=)"-ity anuloman navanītenābhyaṅkte
 6.1.2.14. "vṛtrasya kanīnikāsi cakṣuSpā asi [N1,57a6] caksur me pāhi(1.2.1.i=)"-iti satūlayā śareṣīkayāñjanasyāṅkte trir dakṣiṇaṃ dvir uttaran
 6.1.2.15. dakṣiṇam anu nirasyati ṅa nidhāvata" iti brāhmaṇam ekaviṃśatyā darbhapuñjīlānāñ [N1,57a7]
 6.1.2.16. "citpatis tvā punātu vākpatis tvā punātv(1.2.1k)" ity enam pavayati saptabhir "devas tvā savitā punātv'' ity acchidreṇa pavitreṇa vasos sūryasya raśmibhir" iti saptabhis.
 6.1.2.17. "tasya te pavitrapate pavitreṇa yasmai kaṃ pune tac chakeyam(1.2.1l=)" iti [N1,57a8] saptabhis sarvais saha purejānam pavayed 
 6.1.2.18. "ā vo devāsa īmaha(1.2.1m-o)" ity enam prapādayati śatyadharmāṇo adhvare, yad vo devā[sa=santam pāhi"-iti.
 6.1.2.19. "pa]vamānas suvarjana" ity [N1,57a9] enam uttareṇa vedin tiṣṭhantam aṣṭarcena pāvamānena-upasthāpayaty  
 6.1.2.20. "añjasā satyam upageṣām" ity apareṇa vedim atyākrāmati 
 6.1.2.21. śuvite mā dhā" iti yajamānāyatana upaviśaty [[N1,57a10]
 6.1.2.22. "ākūtyai prayuje 'agnaye svāhā(1.2.2a,b1)"-iti sruvena dhītasya yajūṃṣi juhoti dhruvāyā adhi "medhāyai manase 'agnaye svāhā, dīkṣāyai=pūṣṇe ['agnaye svāhā-, āpo]  devīr [N1,57b1] bṛhatīr viśvaśambhuva" iti samasyati.
 6.1.2.23. "dyāvāpṛthivī urv antarikṣaṃ bṛhaspatir no haviṣā vṛdhātu svāhā(1.2.2b2-3)"-iti sruveṇa hutvā dhruvām āpyāyya juhvāñ catur vigṛhṇāty  
 6.1.2.24. abhipūrya pūrṇāhutiñ juhoti "viśve devasya netur marto vṛṇīta=puṣyase svāhā''-iti (1.2.2c) 
 6.1.2.25. tūṣṇīm antarvedi praṇītā ninīya-antarvedi kṛṣṇājine āstṛṇāti pratīcīnagrīve uttaralomnī 
 6.1.2.26. tayor "ṛksāmayoś śilpe stha(1.2.2d)" iti śuklañ ca kṛṣṇañ ca sammṛśatas "te vām ārabhe te mā pātam āsya yajñasya-udṛca" ity 
 6.1.2.27. atha-ene prasalai prācī paryūhaty anucchindan bhūmer adhīte
 6.1.2.28. "imān dhiyaṃ [N1,57b4] śikṣamāṇasya deva(1.2.2e+1.5.11t)"-iti dakṣiṇaṃ jānv ācya yajamāna āsarpati "kratun dakṣaṃ varuṇa=nāvaṃ ruhema, sutrāmāṇam pṛthivīn dyām anehasaṃ=svastaya" ity 
 6.1.2.29. upari kṛṣṇājine prāgāvṛtta upasthaṃ kṛtvā-upaviśaty  [N1,57b5]
 6.1.2.E. "ūrg asy āṅgirasi"-iti mekhalām paryasyate  

6.1.3 [N1,57b5; M160;T1,189]  Cf.bśs6.5
 6.1.3.1. "ūrg asy āṅgirasi(1.2.2f1=)"-iti mekhalām paryasyata "ūrṇamradā ūrjam (C74) me yaccha"-iti 
 6.1.3.2. " pāhi mā mā hiṃsīr(1.2.2f2=)" iti granthiṃ karoti [N1,57b6]
 6.1.3.3. "viṣṇoś śarmāsi(1.2.2g=)"-iti vāsasā prorṇute "śarma yajamānasya śarma me yaccha"-iti  
 6.1.3.4. "dyāvāpṛthivī prorṇvāthām(?)" iti saśirāḫ prorṇute 
 6.1.3.5. ṅaksatrāṇām mātīkāśāt pāhi(1.2.2h=)"-iti vāsaso 'atīkāśair atīkṣata  [N1,57b7]
 6.1.3.6. "indrasya yonir asi mā mā hiṃsīr(1.2.2i=)" ity asya sici kṛṣṇaviṣāṇām prabadhnāti 
 6.1.3.7. "kṛṣyai tvā susasyāyā(1.2.2k=)" ity antarvedi trir likhati.
 6.1.3.8. śupippalābhyas tvā-oṣadhibhya(1.2.2l=)" ity [N1,57b8] asya dakṣiṇaṃ godānaṁ syute 
 6.1.3.9. śūpasthā devo vanaspatir(1.2.2m=)" ity asmā audumbaran daṇḍaṃ prayacchaty "ūrdhvo mā pāhy ā-udṛca" iti.
 6.1.3.10. śtuto 'asi janadhās sabhakṣo mā mā pāhi"-ity asmā ariktañ camasam ādadhāti  [N1,57b9] 
 6.1.3.11. vrīhiyavau ca bhavata ājyastokaś cāpāñ ca stoko hastāv evātaḫ pratapate tapo.  
 6.1.3.12. "'ahan dīkṣām upaimi"-ity satyaṁ svaṁ śraddhān dakṣiṇām annam akṣitim brahmalokam iti  [N1,58a1]
 6.1.3.13. "prāṇena satyena dīkṣa" iti trir uktvā camase 'adhy aṅgulīr nipicati śvāhā yajñam manasā svāhā dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṁ svāhā-uror antarikṣāt svāhā(cf.1.2.2n)"-iti  
 6.1.3.14. sarvā nipicya "vātād yajñam ārabha(1.2.2n, last)" iti catasra utpicaty [N1,58a2] 
 6.1.3.15. atra kṛṣṇājine adhyūhata udīcīnagrīve "dīkṣito 'asi"-ity enam adhvaryur āha "dīkṣitavādaṃ vada kṛṣṇājinān mā niryauSīr audumbaran te daṇḍas spaṣṭo 'astu mā purā nakṣatrebhyo vācaṃ visṛṣṭā [N1,58a3] yadi visṛjāsai vaiṣṇavīm ṛcam anubrava" iti   
 6.1.3.16. sa yadi pramatto visṛjeta vaiṣṇavīm ṛcam anubrūyād "viṣṇo tvan no antamaś śarma yaccha sahantya pra te dhārā madhuścyuta [N1,58a4] utsan dhrate akṣitam" ity.
 6.1.3.17. ''adīkṣiṣṭāyam brāhmaṇa" iti trir upāṁśv āhādhvaryur antaśśālam asāv āmuṣyāyaṇas tam indrāgnibhyāṃ viśvebhyo devebhyo brāhmaṇebhyas somapebhyaḫ  prabrūma" ity [N1,58a5] 
 6.1.3.18. ''adīkṣiṣṭāyam brāhmaṇa'' iti trir anya uccair niṣkramya-''asāv āmuṣyāyaṇas sa indrāgnibhyāṃ viśvebhyo devebhyobrāhmaṇebhyas somapebhyas somam prāha''-iti
 6.1.3.19. vācaṃyama āsta onnakṣatrād 
 6.1.3.20. uditeṣu nakṣatreṣu pūrvayā dvārā śālāyai niṣkramya "bhūr bhuvas suvar" iti vācaṃ visṛjate "bhūr bhuvas suvar vrataṃ kṛṇuta vrataṃ kṛṇuta vrataṃ kṛṇutāgnir yajñiya [N1,58a7] āpo yajñiyā gāvo yajñiyā devā yajñiyā yajño yajñiyo 'ahaṃ yajñiya'' iti prapadya yajamānāyatana upaviśati  
 6.1.3.21. tat tasmai vratam āharanti 
 6.1.3.22. ''daivīn dhiyam manāmaha'' iti  [N1,58a8] mārjayate śumṛḍīkām abhiṣṭaye varcodhāṃ yajñavāhasaṃ supārā no asad vaśa'' ity  
 6.1.3.23. "ayan te yonir ṛtviya" ity ātmann agniṃ samārohayate [N1,58a9] "yato jāto=vardhaya rayim" iti  
 6.1.3.24. "ye devā manojātā manoyuja" iti vratayati śudakṣā dakṣapitāra=namas tebhya svāhā''-ity 
 6.1.3.25. ''ahan tvad asmi"-iti vratayitvā visṛjati "mad a[si tvam etan mamās]i yoni=yonir asmi yathāyathaṃ vaha havyāni devān putraḫ pitre lokakṛj jātaveda" iti  
 6.1.3.26.  "daivīn dhiyan manāmaha"(1.2.3.1a [pr]) ity evam mārjayate.
 6.1.3.27. saṃviśantam evāto vācayaty ""agne tvam  

6.1.4 [N1,58b1; M162;T1,191]
 6.1.4.1. "agne tvaṃ sujāagṛhi vayaṃ=prabudhe naḫ punar dada" iti 
 6.1.4.2. "tvam agne vratapā asi(1.2.3d1)"-iti prabuddhayajuṣaṃ vācayati ''deva ā martyeṣv ā tvaṃ yajñeṣv īḍya(1.2.3d3)" iti  
 6.1.4.3. "viśve devā abhi mām āvavṛtrann(1.2.3e1)" iti paryāvartasya yajur vācayati [N1,58b2]śam aham indriyeṇa vīryena"-ity.
 6.1.4.4. evam evāsmai prātas taptvā vratam āharanti śamānaṃ vratanam purād" ity 
 6.1.4.5. asya-udayād vācam upākaroty "agnīñ jyotiṣmataḥ kuruta dīkṣita vācaṃ yaccha [N1,58b3]patni vācaṃ yaccha"-iti  
 6.1.4.6. yadi patnī dīkṣitā syād ādityena vācaṃ visṛjyātha yadi saniṃ prahiṇuyāt "pūṣā sanyā(1.2.3e2)"-iti vrajantam anumantrayate śomo ..1.2.3.e .. vasudāvā"-iti  [N1,58b4]
 6.1.4.7. "rāsveyat somābhūyo bhara(1.2.3f=)"-ity āharantiam pratīkṣate "mā pṛṇan pūrtyā virādhi māham āyuṣā(1.2.3g)"-iti.
 6.1.4.8. "candram asi mama bhogāya (C75)  bhava(1.2.3h1)"-iti [N1,58b5] hiraṇyam pratigṛhṇāti, "vastram asi mama bhogāya bhava(h2)" iti vāsa, "usrāsi mama bhogāya bhava(h3)" ity gāṃ, "hayo 'asi mama bhogāya bhava(h4)"-ity aśvaṃ,  "hayataro 'asi [N1,58b6] mama bhogāya bhava(?)"-ity aśvataram, "chāgo 'asi mama bhogāya bhava(h5)"-iti chāgaṃ, "meṣo 'asi mama bhogāya bhava(h6)"-iti mesaṃ, "kṣibastir asi mama bhogāya bhava(?)"-iti hastinaṃ [N1,58b7] vā puruṣaṃ vā, "pataṅgo 'asi mama bhogāya bhava(?)"-iti rathan, "dhuryam asi mama bhogāya bhava(?)"-ity ano vāgāraṃ vā, "pinvitam asi mama bhogāya bhava(?)"-iti kṣīraṃ [N1,58b8] vā sarpir vā-, "-oṣadham asi mama bhogāya bhava(?)"-iti dhānyaṃ, "vānaspatyam asi mama bhogāya bhava(?)"-iti yat kiñ cid vānaspatyam  evam eva-etāni yathādevataṃ pratigṛhṇāti  [N1,58b9]
 6.1.4.9. sa yadi dakṣiṇānāṃ samudākṛtānāṃ pra vā paten naśyed vā tām eṣṭavai brūyāt  
 6.1.4.10. tāṃ yadi na vindeyur "vāyave tvā(1.2.3i1-2)"-iti tām anudiśed 
 6.1.4.11. "varuṇāya tvā"-iti tāṃ yāpsu vā mriyeta [[N1,58b10] pāśo vā hanyān 
 6.1.4.12. ṅirṛtyai tvā(i3)"-iti tāṃ yā saṃ vā śīryetāva vā sīdet kartaṃ vā prapated 
 6.1.4.13. "rudrāya tvā(i4)"-iti tāṃ yām pauruṣo vā vadho hanyān mahādevo vā [N1,59a1] 
 6.1.4.14. "mṛtyave tvā(?)"-iti tāṃ yānaddhakam mriyeta 
 6.1.4.15. sā yā-iyaṃ prapatitā syāt tāṃ yadi vindeyur na-enān dakṣiṇāsv apy upākuryān na goṣṭhe prativeśāṃ rakṣitavai brūyāt  
 6.1.4.16. tān dakṣiṇānāṃ kāle [N1,59a2] 'anṛtvije brāhmaṇāya dadyāt tat tasyai karma 
 6.1.4.17. sa yadi dīkṣitaṃ yoga upeyāt taptvā vratāani vatsān sambadhya samārohyāgnīn "bhadrād abhi śreyaḫ prehi(1.2.3m=)"-iti prayāyād [N1,59a3] "atha-im avasya(1.2.3n2)"-ity avasyed  
 6.1.4.18. ete  eva dīkṣitasya yogāvasāne 
 6.1.4.19. sa yadi vahantīr apo 'adhigacchet tā enaṃ saṃ vā vaheyus saṃ vā hareyur   netv apo 'avakrāmed. 
 6.1.4.20. dvau loṣṭāv ādadīta [N1,59a4] 
 6.1.4.21. tayor anyataram apsu prāsyed "devīr āpo apān napād ya=vo māvakramiṣam'' (1.2.3.k) ity  
 6.1.4.22. "acchinnan tantum pṛthivyā anugeṣam(1.2.3l=)" ity anyataraṃ samuhyamāno vā saṁhriyamāṇo vā viśātayed.
 6.1.4.23. yal loṣṭasyocchiṣyeta [N1,59a5]tat pāre nyasyādhitiṣṭhed "asyai mā yoṣam(?)" iti. 
 6.1.4.24. sa yatra-evāvadhṛtaṃ yakṣyamāṇa syāt tad etāni karmāṇi kuryāt  
 6.1.4.25. tad devayajanam abhimantrayeta  tad udajayet  tat stambāhutiñ juhuyāt   [N1,59a6]
 6.1.4.26. sa yadi dīkṣito 'amedhyam paśyed "abaddham mana(3.1.1.2)" ity enaṃ vācayed "daridraṃ daridrañ cakṣus sūryo jyotiṣāṁ śreṣṭho dīkṣe mā mā hāsīr" ity  
 6.1.4.27. atha yady enam abhivarṣed "undatīr balan dhatta(3.1.1.3a  var.)"-ity [N1,59a7] enaṃ vācayed "ojo dhatta balan dhatta,-ojodā stha baladā mā me dīkṣām mā tapo nirvadhiṣṭha"-iti 
 6.1.4.28. sa yadi dīkṣito 'aśrukuryāt 12-vasthā āpa stha [N1,59a8] svāhākṛtā  pṛthivīṃ gacchata"-iti tā anumantrayeta  
 6.1.4.29. yadi snihānaṃ kuryān "mudā āpa stha svāhākṛtāḫ pṛthivīṃ gacchata"-iti. 
 6.1.4.30. tā yadi sebhuṃ kuryād "bībhatsā āpa stha [N1,59a9] svāhākṛtāḫ pṛthivīṃ gacchata"-iti  
 6.1.4.31. tā yadi svedam prapatet "tapasyā āpa stha svāhākṛtāḫ pṛthivīṃ gacchata"-iti  
 6.1.4.32. tā yadi rudhiram utpatet ''kravyā āpa stha svāhākṛ[tāḫ pṛthivīṃ gaccha]ta''-iti [N1,59b1] 
 6.1.4.33. tā yadi mūtraṃ vā purīṣaṃ vā kuryād "aṃhomuca āpa stha svāhākṛtāḫ pṛthivīṃ gacchata"-iti   
 6.1.4.34. tā yadi retas siñcyād "retassica āpa stha svāhākṛtāḫ pṛthivīṃ gacchata"-iti   [N1,59b2]
 6.1.4.35. tā eṣā dīkṣā-etad dīkṣitasya caraṇam  
 6.1.4.36. api ha-enenāpratipadan dīkṣita syāt   


6.2.1 [N1,59b2; M164;T1,194]
 6.2.1.1. "śvo rājñaḥ kraya" iti rohitaṃ carma palpūlayitvā-uttaraloma (C76) vighnanty  
 6.2.1.2. uṣṇīṣacaturthāni vāsāṃsi saṃbharati cchāgā ca candrañ ca dve ca ūrṇāstuke śuklāni ca kṛṣṇāni cāspandyā ca rājāsandī ca somavāhanañ ca somavāhanau cānaḍvāhau rājakrayaṇī ca [N1,59b4] rājakrayaṇāś ca ṛṣabhaś ca mithunau cānaḍvāñ ca dhenuś ca vāsaś ca catuśśarāvaś ca sarpiśśarāvaś ca balbajastukā ca  
 6.2.1.3. prātar ādityena vācaṃ visṛjya yajñāyudhāni saṃsādyāpaḫ praṇīya prāyaṇīyam nirvapati [N1,59b5] "devasya tvā savituḫ prasave 'aśvinor bāhubhyām pūṣṇo hastābhyām adityai pathyavatyā agnivatyai somavatyai savitṛvatyai juṣṭan nirvapāmi"-iti [N1,59b6] caturo muṣṭīn vrīhiṇān 
 6.2.1.4. nirupte prāṇagrahāṃś ca pṛśnīṃś ca vyācaṣṭe.

 6.2.1.6. apreṇa śālām pariśrita eta eva dīkṣaṇīyās sambhārā upakLptā bhavanty udakumbho 'ahataṃ vāsas sadyomathitan navanītaṃ sadyaḫpiṣṭam āñjanaṃ satūlā śareṣikā-ubhayāni darbhapuñjīlāni kṣuraḫ  
 6.2.1.7.  prapādyaitat pariśritam ''āpa undantu jīvasa(1.2.1.1a)" ity asyai dakṣiṇam upapakṣam anakti "dīrghāyutvāya varcasa" ity  
 6.2.1.8. "oṣadhe trāyasva-enām(1.2.1b=)" iti [N1,59b9] tribhir darbhapuñjīlaiḫ prācīnatūlair adhinidadhāti   
 6.2.1.9. śvadhite mā-enāṃ hiṃsīr(1.2.1c=)" iti svadhitinā "devaśrūr etāni pravapa"-iti pravapati

 6.2.1.14. sravantīr  anvavekṣata ''ud ābhyaś śucir ā puta emi(1.2.1f2)"-ity udīcy utsarpati.

6.2.1.15. ''mahīnām payo 'asi varcodhā asi varco mayi dhehi''-ity anuloman navanītenābhyaṅkte 
6.2.1.16. ''vṛtrasya kanīnikāsi cakṣuSpā asi cakṣur me pāhi''-iti satūlayā śareṣikayāñjanasyāṅkte trir dakṣiṇan dvir uttaran 
6.2.1.17. dakṣiṇam anu nirasyati [N1,60a3] ''na nidhāvata'' iti brāhmaṇam ekaviṃśatyā darbhapuñjīlānān 
6.2.1.18. ''citpatis tvā punātu vākpatis tvā punātv'' ity enam pavayati saptabhir ''devas tvā savitā punātv'' ity acchidreṇa pavitreṇa vasos sūryasya raśmibhir'' iti saptabhis 
6.2.1.19. ''tasya te pavitrapate pavitreṇa yasmai kaṃ pune tac chakeyam'' iti saptabhis sarvais saha purejānasya jāyāṃ pavayet

 6.2.1.21. āgatyāpareṇa gārhapatyaṃ prāg āvṛtta upaviśati [N1,60a6] śan tvāa nahyāmi(3.5.6a)"-ity enāṃ yoktreṇa saṃnahyati "payasā ghṛtena san tvā nahyāmy apa oṣadhībhis san tvā nahyāmi prajayāham adya sā dīkṣitāsanavo vājam asma" iti
 6.2.1.22. śuprajasas tvā vayam(3.5.6d)" ity [N1,60a7] enām upaniṣadaṃ vācayati śupatnī=adābhyam" iti

6.2.3 [N1,61b3; M166; T1,198]
 6.2.3.1. uttiṣṭhan saṃpreṣyati śphyam udapātraṃ paddharaṇīm āharehi yajamānānayantu patnīṃ purastād dvāraṃ vyavasyata"-iti

 6.2.3.3. yat prāyaṇīyena caranty [N1,61b4] etasmin kāle 'agnīd antarvedi praṇītā ninīya carusthālīm etān nirṇijya niṣkāsabhājanam asyān taṇḍulān opya vedam mekṣaṇam barhiṣā-upasaṃnahya madhyame [N1,61b5] sthūṇārāje 'anuveṣṭya nidadhāti

 6.2.3.12. "bṛhaspatis tvā sumne raṇvatv(1.2.5b=)" iti saptamam padam abhigṛhṇāti

 6.2.3.15. "parilikhitaṃ rakṣah parilikhitā arātaya(1.2.5d,e)" iti sphyena pade hiraṇyam abhi parilikhati-"idam ahaṃ [N1,62a1] rakṣaso grīvā apikṛntāmi."-ity 
 6.2.3.16. "asme rāya(1.2.5f1)" iti kṛṣṇaviṣāṇayā yavanmūkaṃ khātvādhvaryuḫ paddharaṇyāṃ saṃvapati

6.3.3 (Cal 6.4.3) [N1,67a9; M179; T1,212; ]
 6.3.3.1. uttaravedyai saṃbhārā.  guggulu sugandhitejanaṃ śuklorṇāstukāḫ pautudravāḫ paridhaya u . . . kṣaṇayaḫ pañcagṛhītam ājyam pañcagṛhītam ājyaṃ [cf.5.1.2.1]
 6.3.3.2. padan tredhā karoty. 
 6.3.3.3. atra-evāgnaye tṛtīyam uparivapaty. upayamanīṣu tṛtīyam akṣopāñjanāyan tṛtīyan nidadhāty. 
 6.3.3.4. ādīpta idhme caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte. taj juhoty [cf.5.1.2.2] [N1,67b1]
 6.3.3.5. "anv agnir uṣasām agram akhyad(4.1.2k)" ity anukhyātim "anv ahāni=ātatāna svāhā"-iti.
 6.3.3.6. hutvā pañcagṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīta.
 6.3.3.7. udyacchanta idhmam  upayacchanty upayamanīr  [cf.5.1.2.7]
6.3.3.8. "agnaye prahriyamāṇāyānubrūhi"-ity āha-"ekasphyānudrava"-iti [N1,67b2]
6.3.3.9. sphyena likhann ekasphyo 'nudravaty ā-uttaravedyai 
6.3.3.10. "kavir yajñasya vitanoti panthām(3.5.5f)" ity adhvaryur yajamānaṃ vācayati 
6.3.3.11  ṅākasya pṛṣthe adhi rocane diva(H) yena havyaṃ vahasi yāsi dūta ita pracetā amukas sanīyān" ity atidrutya-"indraghoṣas tvā vasubhiḫ purastāt pātv(? + 1.2.12i )" ity uttaranābhim prokṣati [N1,67b3] "manojavās tvā pitṛbhir dakṣiṇataḫ pātu pracetās tvā rudraiḫ paścāt pātu viśvakarmā tvādityair uttarataḫ pātu, bṛhaspatis tvā viśvair devair upariṣṭāt pātv" iti 
6.3.3.12.  yat prokṣanīnām ucchiṣyate tad dakṣiṇato mahāvadyai sphyasya vartmann upanīnayati.  [cf.5.1.2.12] [N1,67b4] 
 6.3.3.13. yan dveṣṭi yajamānas tan dhyāyaty 
6.3.3.14.  apa upaspṛśya pañcagṛhītam ādāya hiraṇyam upāsyākṣNayottaranābhim vyāghrāyati ''siṃhīr asi sapatnasāhī svāhā''-iti dakṣiṇe 'Mse, ''siṃhīr asi suprajāvani svāhā''-ity uttarasyāṁ śroṇyāṃ, ''siṃhīr asi rāyaspoṣavani svāhā''-ity dakṣiṇāyāṁ śroṇyāṃ, ''siṃhīr asy ādityavani svāhā''-ity uttare 'Mse, ''siṃhīr asy [N1,67b6] āvaha devān devayate yajamānāya svāhā''-iti madhye. [cf.5.1.2.13] 
6.3.3.15. ''bhūtebhyas tvā''-iti srucam udgṛhṇāti 
6.3.3.16. "viśvāyur asi pṛthivīn dṛMha"-iti madhyamaṃ paridhiṃ paridadhāti pautudravan
6.3.3.17. ''dhruvakṣid asy antarikṣan dṛMha"-iti dakṣiṇārdhyam,
6.3.3.18. "acyutakṣid asi divan dṛMha"-ity uttarārdhyam, 
6.3.3.19. "agner bhasmāsy agneḫ purīṣam asi"-iti saṃbhārān nivapati. 
6.3.3.20.  tān "iḍāyai sṛptaṃ ghṛtavac carācaram" iti saṁsrāveṇābhijuhoti "devā anvavindan guhāhitaṃ ye grāmyāḫ paśavo viśvarūpā virūpās santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ, [N1,67b9] teṣāṃ saptānām mayi rantir astu svāhā''-ity 
6.3.3.21. upanivapaty upayamanīs 
6.3.3.22. sādayaty agnim 
6.3.3.23. "prajāpatir asi viśvakarmā"-iti sannam upatiṣṭhate "tad asau sūryas tad ayam agnis sampriyaḫ paśubhir bhuvad" ity.
6.3.3.24. āhutiṣāhe 'atimuktīr juhoty "agnir yajñan nayatu prajānan mainaṃ yajñahano vidan devebhyaḫ prabrūtād yajñaṃ prapra yajñapatin tira svāhā, vāyur yajñan nayatu prajānan mainaṃ=tira svāhā, sūryo yajñan nayatu prajānan mai=svāhā, yajño yajñan nayatu prajānan mainaṃ yajñahano vidan devebhyaḫ prabrūtād yajñaṃ prapra yajñapati tira svāhā"-ity  [N1,68a1]
 6.3.3.25. avaruhya sphyam ādadāna āha "vyutkrāmata mā vo 'abhiparigrahīṣam brahmann uttaram parigrāham parigrahīṣyāmi"-iti.
 6.3.3.26. dakṣiṇato mahāvedyai brahma-upaviśati.  [cf.5.1.2.31] [N1,68a2]

 6.3.3.29. āsādayanty asmai prokṣaṇīr upasadayatīdhmābarhir. [N1,68a3] 
6.3.3.30. idhmābarhiḫ prokṣati ,vedim prokṣaty, asannam prokṣati, tṛṇakalāpīm proksati.

6.3.3.32. tena sa edhān prokṣaty. 
 6.3.3.33. ādatta etaṃ śākalam idhman.

 6.3.3.E. havirdhāne evātas saṃvartayanti  

6.3.4 (Cal 6.5.1) [N1,68a,5; M180;T1,214; C85]
 6.3.4.1. havirdhāne evātas samvartayanti  dakṣiṇata eva dakṣiṇam  uttarata uttaran  na pṛṣṭhyām vyativartayanti 
 6.3.4.2. te purastāc chālāyai sāṃkāśine sthāpayanti. baddham avasyanti

 6.3.4.4. evaṃ saṃkLpte 'adhvaryuḫ prapadya śālāñ caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte. 
 6.3.4.5. taj juhoti "yuñjate mana [N1,68a7]uta yuñjate dhiya(1.2.13a + svāhā)" ity āhavanīye "viprasya=ariṣṭuti svāhā"-iti.

 6.3.4.7. śruvaṁ srucam" iti sampreṣyati barhir ājyam upāṃśugrahaṇam āharehi yajamānānayantu patnīm" ity.
 6.3.4.8. ānayati neṣṭā patnīn. tān dakṣiṇayā dvārā-upanirhṛtya [N1,68a9] daksiṇenāanvāhāryapacanaṃ padaṃ bibhratīn.   [?]
 6.3.4.9. tasyām āgatāyām adhvaryuś caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte. taj juhoti-. 
 6.3.4.10. "idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrama(1.2.13e + svāhā)" iti dakṣiṇasya havirdhānasya-uttarasyāṃ vartanyāṃ hiraṇyam upāsya "tredhā nidadhe=pāṃsure svāhā"-iti pade saṁsrāvam avanayaty.
 6.3.4.11. "ā no vīro jāyatāṃ karmaṇya(1.2.13d)" iti prācā hastena padenākṣaṃ patny upānakti "yaṃ sa . . . nām asad vaśī"-iti. tūṣṇīn dakṣiṇata upānakty. 
 6.3.4.12. atra-eva sruvaṁ srucaṃ ca barhir ājyam upāṃśugrahaṇam ity atyūhati. 
 6.3.4.13. pratiprasthātre pratipathan neṣṭā patnīṃ nayati. tāṃ dakṣiṇayā dvārā prapādya-uttarāyā niṣkrāmayati. 
 6.3.4.14. tasyām āgatāyāṃ pratiprasthātā caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte. taj juhoti.
 6.3.4.15. -"irāvatī dhenumatī hi bhūtam(1.2.13f+ svāhā)" ity uttarasya havirdhānasya dakṣiṇāyāṃ vartanyāṃ hiraṇyam upāsya śūyavasinī=mayūkhai svāhā"-iti pade saṁsrāvam avanayaty.
 6.3.4.16. "ā no vīro jāyatāṃ karmaṇya(1.2.13d)" ity eva prācā hastena padenākṣaṃ patny upānakti "yaṃ sarve=vaśī"-iti. tūṣṇīm uttarata upānakti.
 6.3.4.17. pratiparaiti patnī. 
 6.3.4.18. "havirdhānābhyāṃ prohyamāṇābhyām anubrūhi"-ity āha.
 6.3.4.19. śuvāg deva duryām āvada(1.2.13b=)"-iti vācayati yadi ca-eva-utsarjati. yadi ca na-"iṣam āvadorjam āvada"-iti [?]
 6.3.4.20. "prācī pretam adhvaraṃ kalpayantī(1.2.13g1)" ity anumantrayata [N1,68b6] "ūrdhvaṃ yajñan nayata mā jīhvaratam" ity.
 6.3.4.21. "atra ramethāṃ varṣman pṛthivyā(1.2.13g2)" iti purastāt parītya kāle nabhyasthe vārayato (C86)

6.3.7 (Cal 6.5.4)[N1,70a10; M185;T1,220;C88]
 6.3.7.1. enān saha sarvān prokṣati. 
 6.3.7.2. "rakṣohaṇo valagahano 'avanayāmi vaiṣṇavān(1.3.2h=)" iti dakṣiṇataḫ pūrvārdhye 'apo 'avanayaty. evam ekasminn evam ekasminn.
 6.3.7.3. "yavo 'asi yavayāsmad dveṣa(1.3.2h=)" iti [N1,70b1] yavān praskandayati "yavāyārātīr" ity.
 6.3.7.4. evam eva-ekasmin evam ekasminn evam ekasmin "raksohaṇo valagahano 'avastṛṇāmi [N1,70b2] vaiṣṇavān(1.3.2k)" iti viṣūcīnāgram barhir  avastṛṇāti.
 6.3.7.5. tasminn eva-evam ekasminn evam ekasmin evam ekasmin "raksohaṇo valagahano 'abhijuhomi vaiṣṇavān svāhā(1.3.2l+svāhā)"-ity enat hiraṇyam antardhāyānyenābhijuhoti.
 6.3.7.6. "rakṣohaṇau valagahanāv upadadhāmi vaiṣṇavī(1.3.2m=)" ity eṣv  adhiṣavaṇaphalake prācī upadadhāti te dvābhyāṃ purastāc chaṃkubhyān dṛMhati  dvābhyāṃ paścād dvābhyāṃ madhye.  [N1,70b4] 
 6.3.7.7. "rakṣohaṇau valgahanau paryūhāmi vaiṣṇavī(1.3.2n=)" ity anena purīṣeṇa paryūhati.
 6.3.7.8. "rakṣohaṇau valagahanau paristṛṇāmi vaiṣṇavī(1.3.2o=)" ity anena barhiṣā-ā ca stṛṇāti pari ca stṛṇāti.  [N1,70b5] 
 6.3.7.9. "rakṣohaṇau valagahanau vaiṣṇavī(1.3.2p)" ity adhivadati.
 6.3.7.10. "raksohaṇaṃ valagahanam adhyūhāmi vaiṣṇavam" ity enayor adhiṣavaṇacarmādhyūhati.
 6.3.7.11. tad "bṛhann asi bṛhadgrāvā"-ity antareṣe dīkṣitadaṇḍena-udvādayati "bṛhatīm indrāya vāraṃ vada, rakṣoghnīṃ valagaghnīṃ vaiṣṇavīm(1.3.2q with addition)" ity.
 6.3.7.12. etasmin kāle pratiprasthātā dakṣinasya havirdhānasya  [N1,70b7] pūrveṇa-upastambhanañ catusraktim prāñcam ākharaṃ kalpayitvā sikatābhiḫ paryūhati. 
 6.3.7.13. śphyam udapātram" iti sampreṣyati "purīṣaṃ sikatā āharehi yajamāna" iti.  [N1,70b8] 
 6.3.7.14. cātvālāt purīṣam āharanti. yatra sikatā vindanti tatas tā āgnīdhriyan dhiṣṇiyam uddhatya vyūhyāvokṣati.
 6.3.7.15. purīṣaṃ nivapati vyūhyāvokṣati. sikatā nivapati. vyūhya-avokṣaty.  [N1,70b9]
 6.3.7.16. evam eva hotur dhiṣṇiyaṃ karoty.  evam maitrāvaruṇasyaivam brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḫ potur neṣṭur acchāvākasya mārjālīyam ity.

6.5.1.39. savyāyai śroṇyai sthūlaṃ piśitam avadyati. 
 6.5.1.40. tad upabhṛty eva-avadadhāti. yūṣṇā-upasiñcati. 
 6.5.1.41. yūṣann avadhāya [N1,76b8] medasā prorṇoti. dvir abhighārayati. na pratyanakti. 
 6.5.1.42. paśuṃ vasāhomaṃ visrāvya-abhighārayaty. 
 6.5.1.43. anasthikam iḍāpātre saṃprakirati. yūṣṇā-upasiñcaty.

6.5.3 (Cal 6.8.3)[N1,77b7; M192;T1,229;C91 ]
 6.5.3.1. tarāṃsi juhoti "ye devā yajñaghana(cf.3.5.4a)" iti dve sruvāhutī gārhapatye juhoty "agnir mā tebhyo rakṣatu gacchema sukṛto vayaṃ svāhā, ye devā yajñamuṣaḥ pṛthivyām adhy āsata agnir mā=vayaṁ svahā"-iti. [2 mantras are condensed in 3.5.4a and mantra word order is different from TS. "pṛthivyām adhi" seems to be missing in C in the first mantra.]
 6.5.3.2. caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte. taj juhoty "āganma .. l.c. b .. divaḥ svāhā(3.5.4b+ svāhā)"-iti.
 6.5.3.3. "ye devā yajñahana(cf.3.5.4c)" iti dve sruvāhutī āgnīdhre juhoty  "antarikṣe 'adhy āsate vāyur .. l.c. c .. vayaṃ svāhā, ye devā yajñamuṣo 'antarikse 'adhy āsate vāyur me .. vayaṃ svāhā"-iti. [2 mantras are condensed in 3.5.4c]
 6.5.3.4. caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte. taj (C92) juhoti "yās te rātrīs  savita=rajāṃsi svāhā(3.5.4d+ svāhā)"-iti.
 6.5.3.5. "ye devā yajñahana(cf.3.5.4e)" iti dve sruvāhutī āhavanīye juhoti "(ye devā yajñahano)[no need!] divy adhy āsate sūryo mā=vayaṁ svāhā, ye devā yajñamuṣo divy adhy āsate sūryo mā=sukṛto vayaṃ svāhā"-iti. [2 mantras are condensed in 3.5.4e, cf. a and c above] [N1.78a1] 
 
 6.5.3.6. caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte. taj juhoti "yena-indrāya=ādhehy enaṁ  svāhā(3,5,4f+svāhā)"-iti.
 6.5.3.7. "vyutkrāmata"-ity āha pratiprasthātā nipadaḥ kāle "mā vo 'abhiparihāriṣaṃ vasatīvarīḥ parihariṣyāmi"-iti.  [ N1,78a2]
 6.5.3.8. pūrvena havirdhāne apareṇāhavanīyaṃ yajamāno rājānan dhārayaṃs tiṣṭhati.  pūrveṇa śālām apareṇa sadaḥ patny upviṣṭā bhavati. 
 6.5.3.9. apareṇa gārhapatyaṃ vasatīvarīs sannā bhavanti. [ N1,78a3] tās tatra pratiprasthātā savyeṇāṃsenādatte. 
 6.5.3.10. tā dakṣiṇena gārhapatyaṃ pūrvayā dvārā-upanirhṛtya dakṣiṇena sado daksiṇaṃ vedyantam anuhṛtya dakṣiṇato yūpasya-aspṛṣṭās sādayati-"indrāgnyor bhāgadheyī stha(1.3.12c1=)"-iti. [N1,78a4] 
 6.5.3.11. tās tato dakṣiṇenāṃsenādatte. tā yathetam āhṛtyāpareṇa gārhapatyaṃ sādayati "mitrāvaruṇayor bhāgadheyī stha(1.3.12c2=)"-iti.
 6.5.3.12. tās tato [N1,78a5]dakṣiṇena-evāṃsenādatte. tā uttareṇa gārhapatyaṃ pūrvayā dvāropanirhṛtya dakṣiṇena sado dakṣiṇenāgnīdhram uttareṇāgnīdhriyam agniṃ gatvā-uttaraṃ [N1,78a6] vedyantam anu hṛtvā-uttarato yūpasyāspṛṣṭās sādayati "viśveṣān devānāṃ bhāgadheyī stha(1,3,12c3=)"-iti.
 6.5.3.13. tās tataḥ savyenaivāṃsenādatte. 
 6.5.3.14. tā yathetam āhṛtyāgnīdhre sādayati "yajñe jāgṛta(1.3.12d=)"-ity. [N1,78a7]
 6.5.3.15. āgnīdhre saṃvasante tadāharati rājānam ādhalanīyapūtabhṛta. eṣām ācchṛṇNā nirṇiktāḥ kṛteṇāvasanty: audumbarā mahāparidhayaḥ parikṛṣtā dvāv idhmāv [N1,78a8]ekaṃ barhis tayor anyataraḫ pañcadaśadāru nānāvārkṣyaḫ prapūtasakalaḫ sapta puṃvṛkṣasya dārūṇy aṣṭau strīvṛkṣyai.
 6.5.3.16. nipadyamānas saṃśāsti "gharmadughaṃ  dadhigharmāya dohatād yā yajamānasya [N1,78a9] vratadhuk payasyāyai yā patniyai tām āśire" iti.
 6.5.3.17. pañcadaśa nakṣatrāṇi rātrin nayanti. 


7.1.1 [N1,78a10;M194;T1,231;C92]
 7.1.1.1. pañcadaśa naksatrāṇi rātrin nayanti. 
 7.1.1.2. yadā daśamam udeti tat tṛtīyam āgataṃ rātriyai bhavati. [N1,78a10]
 7.1.1.3. tasmin kāle samutthāya joṣaṃ kṛteṣu rājānaṃ prapādayati dakṣiṇam abhi havirdhānam. [N1,78b1] 
 7.1.1.4. atha saṃpreṣyaty "agnīd ājyam adhiśraya pratiprasthāta srucas saṃmṛḍḍhy, ājyāni gṛhṇīṣva, [N1,78b2] pātrāṇi yunṅdhy, unnetar grāvNo vāyavyāni daśāpavitre droNakalaśe samavaśāmya havirdhānayos saṃsādaya payaś ca saktūṁś ca-upakalpayāgnīt savanīyān nirvapa sarvān aindrān ekādaśakapālān" ity. 
 7.1.1.5. etat kurvanty. 
 7.1.1.6. srucas saṃmṛjyājyāni gṛhṇīte pratiprasthātāgnīdhre pṛṣadājyavanti.
 7.1.1.7. drutvā khare pātrāṇi yunakty "agnir devatā gāyatrī chanda(3.1.6c1)" iti. [N1,78b4] 
 7.1.1.8. dakṣiṇārdhapūrvārdha ākharasya-upāṃśupātraṃ sādayaty "upāṃśos pātram asi"-iti. 
 7.1.1.9. śomo devatā triṣṭup chanda(3.1.6c2)" iti taduttarenāntaryāmapātraṃ sādayaty "antaryāmasya pātram asi"-ity. [N1,78b5] 
 7.1.1.10. antareṇa grāvāṇam āspṛṣṭaṃ sādayati dakṣiṇāmukham upāṃśu savanam. 
 7.1.1.11. "indro devatā jagatī cchanda(3.1.6c3)" iti tasya madhyato 'apareṇāspṛṣṭaṃ parisrajam aindravāyavaṃ pātraṃ (C93) sādayati-"indravāyuvoḫ pātram asi"-iti.
 7.1.1.12. "bṛhaspatir devatānuṣṭup cchanda(3.1.6c4)" iti tadapareṇāspṛṣṭan dvibhṛṣṭi maitrāvaruṇapātraṃ [N1,78b7] sādayati "mitrāvaruṇayoḫ pātram asi"-ity.
 7.1.1.13. "aśvinau devatā paṅktiś chanda(3.1.6c5)" iti tadapareṇāspṛṣṭaṁ stanavad  āśvinam pātraṃ sādayaty ''aśvinoḫ pātram asi''-iti  [N1,78b8]
 7.1.1.14. śūryo devatā bṛhatī cchanda(3.1.6c6)" iti tasya dakṣiṇato 'apareṇa bailvaṃ śukrapātraṃ sādayati "śukrasya pātram asi"-iti.
 7.1.1.15. "candramā devatā satobṛhatī chanda(3.1.6c7)" iti taduttareṇa vaikaṅkatam manthipātraṃ [N1,78b9] sādayati "manthinaḫ pātram asi"-ity.
 7.1.1.16. apareṇa śukrāmanthinoḫ pātre āśvatthe aśvaśaphabudhne ubhayatomukhe prasthite prabāhu gṛtapātre sādayaty "ṛtūnām pātre stha" iti. [N1,78b10] 
 7.1.1.17. "viśve devā devatā-uṣṇihā chanda(3.1.6c8)" iti dakṣiṇasya havirdhānasya pūrvena-upastaṃbhanam āgrayaṇasthālīṃ sādayaty "āgrayaṇasya pātram asi''-iti- 
 7.1.1.18. ''indro devatā kakuc chanda iti" [N1,79a1] dakṣiṇato 'asyā ukthyasthālīṃ sādayaty   "ukthānāṃ pātram asi(3.1.6c9, latter part)"-ity. 
 7.1.1.19. upanidadhaty asyā upaśayaṃ pātram.
 7.1.1.20. uttarato 'asyā ādityasthālīṃ sādayaty "ādityānāṃ pātram asi(?)"-ity.
 7.1.1.21. upanidadhaty asyā auduṃbaram ādityapātrañ catusrakti. 
 7.1.1.22. "pṛthivī devatā virāṭ chanda(3.1.6c10)" ity apareṇātmānaṃ hṛtvā-uttarasya havirdhānasya pūrveṇa-upastaṃbhanam anupopte dhruvasthālīṃ sādayati "dhruvasya pātram asi"-iti.

 7.1.2.19. śoma rājann ehy avaroha(1.3.13c-f)"-ity antareṣe rājānam upāvaharati "mā bher mā=upāvarohantv" ity.

 7.1.2.23. 'apāvṛtya tisqNām ṛcām manasā pratyāgṛṇāti. 
 7.1.2.24. saṃsṛpyāste ṅimeṣa etasya-ukthyasya pratigara" ity āhur. (C94)
 7.1.2.25. "yat prātaranuvāka" iti yada jānāty "abhūd uṣā ruśat paśur agnir adhāyy ṛtviyaḥ ayoji vāṃ vṛṣaṇvasū ratho dasrāv amartyo mādhvī mama śrutaṃ havam(?)" ity. 
 7.1.2.26. athādhvaryuḫ pracaraṇyāñ caturgṛhītam gṛhṇīte. taj juhoti "śṛṇotv agnis samidhā havaṃ ma(1.3.13g+svāhā)" ity anuvittim āhavanīye juhoti"śṛṇvantv āpo=havaṃ ma svāhā"-ity.[anuvitti=TS1.3.13g]
 7.1.2.27. aparañ caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte barhir ādāya-uttiṣṭhan saṃpreṣyati || 2 || 

7.1.3 [N1,79b6; M196;T1,234; ]
 7.1.3.1. uttiṣṭhan saṃpreṣyaty "apa iṣya hotar maitrāvaruṇasya camasādhvaryor ādrava-ekadhanina ādravata neṣṭaḫ patnīm [N1,79b7]udānayāgnīdd hotṛcamasena ca vasatīvarībhiś ca cātvāle pratitiṣṭhāsā" ity.
 7.1.3.2. [apa] ānayati. neṣṭā patnīṃ saha pānnejanīyayā sthālyā "prehy udehy ṛtasya vāmī=mā mā hiṃsīr(3.5.6f)" ity antarā cātvālañ ca-utkarañ ca gatvā vahantīr āgacchanti. 
 7.1.3.3. tad barhiḫ prāsyābhijuhoti "devīr āpo apān napād ya=ūrmir bhāga stha svāhā(1.3.13h)"-iti. [N1,79b9] 
 7.1.3.4. "kārṣir asi(1.3.13i)"-iti maitrāvaruṇacamasam ādatte 
 7.1.3.5. "'apāpām mṛdhram" iti drapsān vihanti 
 7.1.3.6. śamudrasya vo 'akṣityā unnaya(1.3.13k=)" ity unnayate. 
 7.1.3.7. tad anv ekadhanān unnayanti-"indrāya va indrāya va(?)" iti. [N1,79b10] 
 7.1.3.8. "vasubhyo rudrebhya ādityebhya(3.5.6.2g1)" iti neṣṭā patnīṃ pānnejanaṃ grāhayati.
 7.1.3.9 "viśvebhyo vo devebhyaḫ pānnejanīr gṛhṇāmi(3.5.6g2)"-ity āyanty.
 7.1.3.10. "ehy udehi(3.5.6f, with var.)"-iti neṣṭā [N1,80a1] patnīm udānayaty "ṛtasya vāmīr anv agnis te 'agram nayatv aditi=nāma namas te astu mā mā hiṃsīr" iti.
 7.1.3.11. tā yathetam āgatyāpareṇa gārhapatyaṃ sādayaty. 
 7.1.3.12. āgacchaty adhvaryur 
 7.1.3.13. hotṛcamasañ ca vasatīvarīś ca cātvāle saṁspṛṣṭā. [N1,80a2] 
 7.1.3.14. hotṛcamase vasatīvarībhyo 'adhi praskandayati. 
 7.1.3.15. tam atiriktan tiraḫ pavitraṃ pracaraṇyā samanakti śaṃ jyotiṣā jyotir aṅktāṃ saṃ haviṣā havis saṃpratair vratacāriṇīr(?)" iti.
 7.1.3.16. saṃpūrayanty enam[N1,60a3]  maitrāvaruṇacamasāc ca vasatīvarībhyaś ca 
 7.1.3.17. yā maitrāvaruṇacamase 'atiśiṣyeran vasatīvarīṣu tā ānīya saṃpūrṇam etaṃ graham abhimṛśataś "śukrā stha śukrajyotiṣaś śukrasya bhartrī jyotiṣmantaṃ [N1,80a4] vo grahaṃ gṛhṇāmi(?)"-iti pūrveṇa nigrābhyāḫ prapādayanty, apareṇa vasatīvarīś ca-ekadhanāś ca-. [?]
 7.1.3.18. -"adhvaryo 'aver apā" iti hotādhvaryuṃ pṛcchati. 
 7.1.3.19. śaṃsrāveṇādravantaṃ utamanannamur" iti sa āha. [N1,80a5] 
 7.1.3.20. yadi na pṛcchet śam agur" iti brūyād.
 7.1.3.21. "yam agne pṛtsu martyam(1.3.13l+svāhā)" ity āhavanīye saṃsrāvañ juhoty "āvo vājeṣu yaṃ junāḥ sa yantā śaśvatīr iṣa svāhā"-iti yady agniṣṭomo juhoti. [N1,80a6]
 7.1.3.22. yady ukthyaḥ paridhau nimārṣṭi. 
 7.1.3.23. yady atirātro hutvā paridhau nimṛjya yajur vadan prapadyate dhuri pracaraṇīṃ sādayitvā-"upayāmagṛhīto 'asi(3.5.8a)"-iti prājāpatyan[N1,80a7] dadhigrahaṃ gṛhṇāty auduṃbareṇādityapātreṇa catusraktinā tiraḫ pavitran dadhnaḫ "prajāpataye tvā jyotiṣmate jyotiṣmantaṃ gṛhṇāmi dakṣāya= pṛthivyai tvā"-ity.
 7.1.3.24. "apendra dviṣato mana(3.5.8b)" ity upayacchaty "apajijyāsato=tañ jahi"-iti. 
 7.1.3.25. "prāṇāya tvā(3.5.8c1)"-ity uttiṣṭhaty. "apānāya tvā(3.5.8c2)"-iti vrajati. "vyānāya tvā sate tvā=jyotiṣmantañ juhomi svāhā(3.5.8c3+svāhā)"-iti parasmai dadāti. [N1,80a9]
 7.1.3.26. yathāyatanaṃ pātraṃ sādayitvā-"agniḫ prātassavane pātv asmān(3.1.9b)" iti vasatīvarīṇān tṛtīyam apanayato 'aparasmin kalaśe "vaiśvānaro mahinā viśva=saha bhakṣās syāma"-iti.
 7.1.3.27. tad anv ekadhanam ānayanti nigrābhyā stha 

7.1.4   [N1,80a10; M198;T1,236; C95]

 7.1.4.5. "śvātrā stha vṛtratura(1.4.1c)" ity [N1,80b4] apa upasṛjati nigrābhyābhyo 'adhi yajamāno "rādhogūrtā ..1.4.1c .. pibatv" iti.

 7.3.1.1. saṃprasarpanty evāta

 7.3.1.3. "upa mā dyāvāpṛthivī hvayetām(3.2.4b1=)" ity dyāvāpṛthivī, [N1,84a2] "upāstāva(b2)" ity[?] āstāvam, "upa kalaśa(cf.b3)" iti kalaśam, "upa soma(cf.b4)" iti somam, "upāgnir(cf.b5)"  ity "agnim, "upa devā(b6)" iti devān, "upa yajña(b7)" iti yajñam, "upa mā hotrā upahave hvayantām(b8=)" iti hotrā.
 7.3.1.4. ṅamo 'agnaye makhaghne makhasya mā yaśo 'aryād(3.2.4c)" ity [N1,84a3] āhavanīyam upatiṣṭhante. 
 7.3.1.5. ṅamo rudrāya makhaghne namaskṛtyā mā pāhi(3.2.4d)"-ity āgnīdhran, ṅama indrāya makhaghne indriyam me vīryam mā nirvadhīr'' iti hotriyan, ''namo yamāya makhaghne makhasya mā yaśo 'aryād''(cf.3.2.4e) iti [N1,84a4]  mārjālīyan.  [cf.3.2.4.2]

 7.3.1.9. ṅirastaḥ paravasur" iti [N1,84a7] tṛṇan nirasyati.-"idam aham āyuṣas sadane sīdāmi"-iti pūrveṇa hotur dhiṣṇiyaṃ pratyagāvṛtta upasthaṃ kṛTvā-upaviśaty.

 7.3.2.9. pratiprasthātuḥ pātre saṃsrāvam ānayati.[N1,84b7] tam u sa ādityasthālyān "devebhyas tvā"-iti drutvā-etat pātraṃ hotra upanidhāyādravann āha "mitrāvaruṇābhyām anubrūhi"-iti.

''vasanta ṛtur mayi āyur dadhātv'' iti [N1,88a3] hotāraṃ, ''grīṣma ṛtur mayi tejo dadhātv'' iti potāraṃ , ''varṣā ṛtur mayi cakṣur dadhātv'' iti neṣṭāraṃ, ''śarad ṛtur mayi śrotran dadhātv'' ity agnīdhaṃ, ''hemanta ṛtur mayi yojo dadhātv'' iti [N1,88a4] brahmaṇācchaṃsinaṃ, ''śiśira ṛtur mayi vācan dadhātv'' iti maitrāvaruṇaṃ paryasyati pātram itarato mukhaṃ karoty 
 7.3.6.3. atha hotāram eva ''saṃvatsaro mayi reto dadhātu prajāvantas [N1,88a5] sarvagaṇā syāma''-ity eva potāram eva neṣṭāram, evam acchāvākaṃ paryasyaty eva pātram itarato mukhaṃ karoty 
 7.3.6.4. atha hotāram eva saṃvatsaro māsu vite dadhātv [N1,88a6] āyuSmantas saha bhakṣā syāma''-ity 
 7.3.6.5. atha hotāram eva parāṅ āvartate 'adhvaryuḫ purastādd hotus saha pātreṇeḍā debahu | manuṣya agnīt pitaro 'anumadantv'' iti yāvad etasya yajuṣo 'abhyāpnoty [N1,88a7]
 7.3.6.6.  abhyāhvayata enaṃ ''śoMsāv om'' iti ''śoMsāmo daiva''-ity adhvaryuḫ pratyuttiṣṭhaty upāṃśu śaṃseM hotā pratipadyate tasya tathā pratyāgṛṇīyād 
 7.3.6.7. o . . mo daivo thāmodeva''-iti ṣaṣṭhena hotā praṇauti ṣṭenaivādhvaryuḫ pranauti  ''pra vo devāyāgnaya'' ity anuṣṭubhā hotā pratipadyate 
 7.3.6.8. tasyai dvi nyasyati pratha . . .ābhyān [N1,88a9] dvir evāsyā adhvaryun nyasyati 
 7.3.6.9. samasyottarābhyāṃ hotā praṇauti  samane asya vottarābhyām adhvaryuḫ praṇauti 
 7.3.6.10. prasidhyati  pratigaro  'anyāse vaiva praṇave . . .(5)ty etad ājyam uktham ukthaśā'' ity uktvādravaty 
 7.3.6.11. ādatta etaṃ graham anūdyacchante camasān 
 7.3.6.12. ''o śrāvaya''-ity āha-ukthaśā yaja somasya''-iti 
 7.3.6.13. vaṣaṭkṛte juhoty  anuvaṣaṭkṛ. . .(5) vrataṃ payanti [N1,88b1] 
 7.3.6.14. camasān na juhvati 
 7.3.6.15. hutañ cābhakṣite 'apanayāmi''-iti hotṛcamase saṁsrāvam avanayaty ''ūrjasvantan devebhyaḫ payasvantam manuṣyebhya''ioti yajamā . . .(4)tara''-iti nidhāyaitat  pātram āyanti 
 7.3.6.16. bhakṣayanti narāśaṃsapītasya soma devatā''-iti pratidadhate ''matividaḫ prātassavanasya gāyatracchandasaḫ pitṛpītasya madhumatā . . syopahūto [N1,88a3] bhakṣayāmi''-iti samupahāvam ucchiṃṣanti rājānan 
 7.3.6.17. tam āpyāyayanty ''āpyāyasva sam etu te=saṃgatha'' iti 
 7.3.6.18. 'san te payāṃsi''-ity eva rājanyasya ''sam u yantu=dhiṣva''-iti [N1,88b4]
 7.3.6.19. sīdanti nārāśaṃsās 
 7.3.6.20. sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣu-''unnetar '' ity āha 

7.3.7 [N1,88b4; T1,259(7.3.6);] 
 7.3.7.1. unnetar'' ity āhādhvaryur ''yademaṃ gṛhaṃ gṛhṇāmy atha śukraṃ pūtabhṛty avanaya''-iti vaiśvadevaṃ śukrapātreṇa gṛhṇāty [N1,88a5] 
 7.3.7.2. ''om āsaś carṣaṇīdhṛto viśve devāsa āgatadāśvāṃ sodāśuṣa snutaṃ upayāmagṛhīto 'asi viśvebhyas tvā devebhyo juṣṭaṃ gṛhṇāmy eṣa te yonir [N1,88a6] viśvebhyas tvā devebhya'' iti daśāpavitreṇa parimṛjya sādayati  hiraṇyeṇa śrīṇāti  
 7.3.7.3. śukraṃ pūtabhṛti unnetāvanayati 
 7.3.7.4. nyubjya kalaśaṃ prastaram ādāya [N1,88b7] stotreṇa dravati ''vāyur hiṃkartā''-ity upākaraṇañ japati ''mayi prāṇā upāvartadhvaṃ yajamānābhisarpa''-iti stuvānān upāstahiṣottamā''-iti jñātāparāṅ asavartate [N1,88b8] 
 7.3.7.5. purastādd hotur iḍā devahūr'' iti yāvad etasya yajuṣo 'abhyāpnoty  abhyāhvayata enaṃ pratyāgṛṇāty 
 7.3.7.6. etat praugam ukthan tasya purastāt tripānān nividś śasyante nyasena tāsāṃ pratyāgīrya nyāsapraṇavābhyān tṛcānāṃ pratyāgṛṇāty 
 7.3.7.7. etad utvam ukthaśā'' ity uktvā dravaty  
 7.3.7.8. ādatta etad agrahamantrabhyaś śan te camasān o śrāvaya''-ity āha-''ukthaśā yaja somasya''-iti 
 7.3.7.9. vaṣaṭkṛte juhoty [N1,88b10] anuvaṣaṭkṛte juhoty 
 7.3.7.10. anuprakaṃpayanti  camasān na juhvati ''hutan tvā bhakṣite 'avanayāmi''-iti hotṛcamase saṁsrāvam avanayaty ''ūrjasvantan devebhyaḫ payasvata [N1,89a1] manuṣyebhya'' iti 
 7.3.7.11. yajamānacamasan nāntarā''-iti nidhāya tat pātram āyanti 
 7.3.7.12. bhakṣayanti bhakṣayanti narāśaṃsaṣi tasya somadevata'' iti sarvabhakṣyāgnīt prasūtā'' iti mārjayante maitrāvaruṇasya [N1,89a2] camasam anūnnayadhvam'' ity āhādravann adhvaryur ''unnetas somaṃ prabhāvaya''-iti gṛhṇīta ukthyasthālyā adhyupaśayena pātreṇa-''upayāmagṛhīto 'asi devebhyas tvā devāyuvam ukthebhya havam [N1,89a3] mitrāvaruṇābhyāñ juṣṭaṃ gṛhṇāmy  eṣa te yonir mitrāvaruṇābhyān tvā''-iti daśāpavitreṇa parimṛjya sādayati hiraṇyena śrīṇāti 
7.3.7.13. ''punar havir asi''-iti sthālīm abhimṛśya prastaram ādāya stotreṇa dravati 
7.3.7.14. ''vāyur hiṅkartā''-iti upākaraṇañ japati ''mayi prāṇā upāvartadhvaṃ yajamānābhisarpa''-iti stuvānān upāsta ''eṣotamā''-iti jñātvā parāṅ āvartate [N1,89a5] 
7.3.7.15. purastān maitrāvaruṇasya-''iḍā devahūr'' iti yāvad etasya yajuṣo 'abhyāpnoty 
7.3.7.16. abhyāhvayata enaṃ pratyagṛṇāty 
7.3.7.17. etad uktham ukthaśā'' ity uktvā dravaty  
7.3.7.18. ādatta etaṃ gṛham anūdyacchānte [N1.89a6] camasān 
7.3.7.19. ''o śrāvaya''-ity āha-''ukthaśā yaja somānām'' iti 
7.3.7.20. vaṣaṭkṛte juhvati 
7.3.7.21. hutañ cāhute 'avanayāmi''-iti maitrāvaruṇacamase saṁsravam avanayaty [N1,89a7] ''ūrjasvantan de=manuṣyebhya'' iti hotṛcamasañ ca yajamānasamanañ ca 
7.3.7.22. ''nāntarā''-iti nidhāyaitat pātram āyanti  
7.3.7.23. bhakṣayanti ''vasumad gaṇasya soma deva . . . sarvabhakṣyāgnīt prasūtā'' iti [N1.89a8] mārjayanta 
7.3.7.24. ''ehi yajamāna''-ity uktvādhvaryuḫ prapadya havirdhāne viśvedevā maruta indro asmān'' iti vasati

7.5.1 [N1,95a1; M219;T1,274]

7.5.1.2.  "ehi yajamāna"-ity uktvādhvaryuḫ prapadya havirdhāne sarpir grīvī  pīvaryasya jāya"-iti tisṛbhir āśiram avanayato 'aparasmin kalaśe pīvānaṃ putrāḥ=sapatnān [N1,95a3] ūrjaṃ suhūtiṃ savitā dadhātu no viśvāhendro adhivaktā no astu aparihūtās sūnuyāma-indrāya soman tan no mitrāvaruṇā mā mahantām aditis sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyaur" ity [N1,95a4]

7.5.1.5.  ''kadā cana starīr asi"-ity [N1,95a5] upayāmam ādityagrahaṃ gṛhṇāty auduṃbareṇādityapātreṇa catusraktinā saṁsrāvāṇāgnedrasaś casi=pṛcyate

7.5.1.9.  ''vivasva āditya''-ity 'asmin grāvāṇaṃ pariplāvayaty upāṃśusavanam ''eṣa te somapīthas tena mandasva=tarpayitāra" ity udgṛhṇāti

7.5.7.38. ''uror ā no deva riṣas pāhi''-iti bindūn pratiyūya ''sumitrā na āpa oṣadhayas santv'' iti prāṇenāpāṃ bhaksayati ''durmitrā=dviṣma'' iti 
7.5.7.39. ''san tvā viśa . . .(6)r utāpa'' ity [N1,100b10] avabhṛthaṃ saṃprakirati ''yajñasya tvā=vidhema''-iti 
7.5.7.40. ''devīr āpa eṣa vo garbha'' iti saṃprakīrṇam  anumantrayate ''tam vas suprītaṃ  subhṛtam akarmaso asmān suprītas subhṛtaḫ pātu deve . . .(6)[o] brūtād'' iti  [N1,101a1]

7.5.7.48. ''samid asi tejo 'asi tejo 'asi tejo mayi dhehi''-ity āhavanīye yajamāno 'abhyādadhāty 7.5.7.49-50. evam eva gārha . . .(8)āriṣam'' ity [N1,101a4] eṣāv upasthāpayati ''rasena=tam mā saṃsṛja varcasā''-iti

7.5.9 [N1,102b1; T1,290]

7.5.9.1. ''ayan te yonir ṛtviya'' ity agniṃ samārohayate ''yato jāto=rayim'' iti 
7.5.9.2. ''yat kusīdam apratīttaṃ mayi"-ity uttarāṃ vedyai śroNim upoṣati "yena yamasya=anṛṇo bhavāmi''-iti 
7.5.9.3. ''viśvalopa viśvadāvasya tvāsañ juhomi''-ity añjalinā [N1,102b3] saktūn pradāsye juhuto yajamānaś ca patnī ca-
7.5.9.4. ''agdhād eka'' iti hastān samprabnāte ''ahutād eka=vareṇyam'' ity 
7.5.9.5. ''ayan no nabhasā pura'' [ity ādityam upatiṣṭhate] ''saṁsphāno =grahā asann'' iti [N1,102b4] 
7.5.9.6. ''sa tvan no nabhasas pata'' iti vāyum ''ūrjan no dhehi=kṛdhi''-iti 
7.5.9.7. ''deva saṁsphāna''-ity  ādityam ''sahasrapoṣasyeśiṣe=svasti[m'' ity 
7.5.9.8. [āśāsānaḥ suvīrya]m'' iti yūpaṃ [N1,102b5] ''rāyas poṣaṁ śviyaṃ yajamānāya tiṣṭha''-iti 
7.5.9.9. sphyam ādāya-udapātrañ ca hṛdayaśūlena-utparāyanty 
7.5.9.10. antarā cātvālañ ca-utkarañ ca gatvā [pūrveṇa yūpaṃ bahi]rv[e]di sphyena-uddhatya-''unnambhaya pṛthivīm'' ity udapātreṇāvokṣati ''bhinddhīda=dṛtim'' iti 
7.5.9.11. ''śug asi''-iti śuṣkasya cārdrasya ca sandhau parāñcaṃ hṛ[dayaśūlam udvāsaya]ti ''tam abhiśoca=tam abhiśoca''-iti 
7.5.9.12. ''dhāmno-dhāmno rājann'' ity upatiṣṭhate ''ito varuṇa no muñca yad āpo aghniyā=m muñca,  apsu rājño varuṇasya gṛ[ho hiraṇmayaḥ kṛ]taḥ [N1,102b8] tato dhṛtavrato rāajā viśvā dharmāṇi paśyati''-iti 
7.5.9.13. ''sumitrā na āpa oṣadhayas santv'' ity apaḫ pariṣicyāpratīkṣam āyanti ''durmitrā=dviṣma'' ity 
7.5.9.14. utparāyanty agnibhir 
7.5.9.15. antarā cātvālañ ca-utkrañ ca [N1,102b9] gatvā trīṇy uddhanāny āyanti 
7.5.9.16. prokṣya-''agner janitram asi''-iti śakalam ādatte  'vṛṣaṇau stha'' iti vṛṣāṇāv  ''urvaśy asi''-ity araṇiṃ  ''purūravāa'' ity uttarāraṇiṃ 
7.5.9.17. ''ghṛtenānakte'' ity anakti 
7.5.9.18. ''vṛṣaṇan dadhāthām'' ity avadadhāti [N1,103a1] 
7.5.9.19. ''gāyatrañ chando 'anu prajāyasva''-iti chandobhir ātmānam abhi nivartayate ''traiṣṭubhañ cha=prajāyasva''-iti 
7.5.9.20. daśahotāram araṇyor vyācaṣṭe 
7.5.9.21. manthanty agnim 
7.5.9.22. [āhutiṣāhe] gārhapatye caturgṛhītena [N1,103a2] daśahotur āhutiñ juhoti 
7.5.9.23. praharaty  āhavanīyan  
7.5.9.24. nidadhāty anvāhāryapacanam 
7.5.9.25. praharaty āhavanīyan nidadhāty anvāhāryapacanam 
7.5.9.26 . āhutiṣāhe gārhapatye ca[turgṛhītena daśa]h[o]tur āhutiñ juhoti [N1,103a3]  saptahotāram āhavanīye ṣaḍḍhotāram anvāhāryapacane pañcahotāram āmātye caturhotāraṃ sabhāyām 
7.5.9.27.  āgatya dvir ājyasya-upastṛṇīte  dadhy ānayati [N1,103a4] dvir evābhighārayati

7.5.9.29. ''punar ūrjā nivartasva''-iti [dadhnā gārhapatye ju]hoti [N1,103a5] ''punar agna iṣā=viśvata svāhā''-iti 
7.5.9.30. ''saha rayyā nivartasva''-iti vayasānvāhāryavacane ''agne pinvasva=viśvatas pari svāhā''-iti 
7.5.9.31. ''samudrād ūrmir madhumāṃ ud āraḍ" ity apsu taṇḍulāñ juhoty [N1,103a6] ''upāṃśunā sam amṛtatvam ānaṭ ghṛtasya nāma guhyaṃ yad asti jihvā devānām amṛtasya nābhi svāhā''-ity 
7.5.9.32. ''āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuva'' iti [darbhastamba udapātrañ juho]ti [N1,103a7] ''tā na ūrje vakṣase svāhā''-iti 
7.5.9.33. ''vanaspate śatavaliśo viroha svāhā''-iti palāśe hiraṇyam upāsya juhoti  
7.5.9.34. pūrṇāhutim evāto juho[ti] 
7.5.9.35.   . . .(8)turgṛhītaṃ gṛhṇīte [N1,103a8]
